2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Term dict and store:** term dict `TermMappingAndGate` (aliases, curator verdicts, gate verdicts, placements, cell decisions in `record["relations"]` with status rejected or repredicated). ConceptStore view: evidence only; decisions are applied in the view.
   - **Pass 0:** groups names by `Normalised.key` / `.strict` and elects a canonical (ontology label first, then frequency). `canonical_vocabulary` holds the per-name rewrites.
   - **Hierarchy:** sources and ranks curated 4 > placed 3 > text 2 > fca 1. ELK edge gate with rank-based quarantine, explanation-guided culprit search, and individuals stripped.
   - **Reasoner skeleton:** WONT + MILD + BFO axioms and RO/BFO/IAO domain/range from the modules. EL weakening of intersections; unions are skipped.
   - **Export and core:**
     - `extend_ground_truth` with `class_edges`, `relations`, `registry`, `former_names`.
     - Core builder: placed edges only, BFO typing, read relations, curator axioms (resolved by name for minted classes), and an ELK settling loop.
   - **ID registry (rule 71):** keyed by `Normalised.key`; consecutive after WONT's max id; obsolete/replaced_by on merges; former names come from aliases but only when they are not live names; next free id written for Protégé.
   - **Reasoners:** ROBOT ELK, HermiT, JFact. OWL 2 EL profile validation.
   - **Pages:** artifact db pages (`claude.use("db")`, collections, JSON copies of frozen docs).

It starts from the 6,029 abstracts and goes through extraction (84,596 names) and naming and scope (32,519 terms). Then it splits into two lanes that join at the WONT addition:

- **Classes:** 7,576 terms named in at least 2 abstracts → 3,129 placed under a WONT, MILD or BFO class → 3,109 placed edges after ELK checks the hierarchy → 7,212 classes given WONT ids (WONT_0000591–7802) → **2,953 core classes**.

- **The result box:** 2,943 placed subclass edges, 1,926 BFO typings, 44 relations, your 42 axioms, and the next free id WONT_0007803. ELK finds 0 unsatisfiable classes.

2026-10-05 14:02:30 · Claude
Written for: readers of the thesis or a paper. Labels follow your thesis wording rules, so no pipeline jargon.

The figure is in [Thesis/figures/](../Thesis/figures/) as `abstracts_to_wont.svg`, `.pdf` and `.png`. The PDF is vector, one page, 180 mm wide (full text width), with fonts embedded. The PNG is at 4× resolution.

**How it's built.** A new script, [scripts/figure_abstracts_to_wont.py](scripts/figure_abstracts_to_wont.py), measures every number itself and saves them to [output/figure_abstracts_to_wont_2026-10-05_09-57.json](output/figure_abstracts_to_wont_2026-10-05_09-57.json). It stops instead of drawing if:
- any box's exclusions don't add up;
- any term that didn't reach the addition has no named reason;
- any line of text doesn't fit its box.

Re-run it after the final rebuild. `--from <json>` redraws without measuring again.

**What changed from the web page.** Measuring properly corrected three things:
- **Extracted strings:** 96,239 term strings as they came out of the model, not 84,596. That old number was taken after spelling variants were already merged and included relation values. The figure no longer claims a subtraction between this box and the next.
- **Placed terms:** 3,114 distinct terms, not 3,129. The 3,129 counted term–parent pairs. Of the 3,114, 169 are not in the addition:
  - 140 because their parent class has no placement of its own (for example, the bacteria under "Bacterium");
  - 24 because they already are a WONT, MILD or imported class;
  - 5 because ELK found every one of their placements inconsistent.
- **Relations:** the 203 universal relations become 195 distinct ones after the naming rulings. 85 fall on existing classes and 110 on new ones. Of the 110, 44 are added; 63 are left out because the subject isn't in the addition, 2 because the value isn't, and 1 as inconsistent.

**Draft caption:**
> **Figure X.** From abstracts to the WONT addition. Grey boxes: excluded at that step. Dashed box: relations on existing classes go to curator review, not into the addition automatically. Of these, 82 were reviewed on 3 October 2026; later naming rulings bring the count to 85. Not shown: 4,259 further classes kept apart as candidates. Together with the 2,953 new classes they make up the 7,212 permanent identifiers issued.

**Problem found on the way.** The normaliser's key is case-sensitive when it drops a final "s": "Retro-Rectus" becomes `retrorectu`, but "retro-rectus" becomes `retrorectus`. The ID registry keys on this, so two spellings of one class can get two identifiers. The figure script uses the strict form and is not affected. Should I fix the key and check the registry for split entries?

HermiT and JFact on the core are still running.

- **"Alginate / Chitosan has quality Material Biocompatibility"** is a claim about a material's properties, not about what every alginate is.

The review page is up: **https://claude.ai/artifact/V6Ejezr8tobgJeiwHgy8uM**. The 45 relations are out of WONT until you decide them. Your decisions are saved as you click.

The page has five tabs, 72 items in all:

| Tab | Items | What you decide |
|---|---|---|
| Relations on new classes | 45 | Add or leave out. Each row shows the abstracts it came from (PubMed links), how the abstracts wrote it, and what I noted when I read it. |
| Possible duplicates | 4 | Whether a new class means the same as one that already exists. For example, "Chronic Skin Wound" vs HP "Chronic cutaneous wound", and "Complete-Thickness" vs WONT "Full-thickness". |
| Waiting for a parent | 10 | 76 classes stay out because their parent has no parent itself, for example the 50 under "Cell Process". Type a parent and they all come in. |
| Contradicted by the reasoner | 5 | Terms like "complete wound closure", where the reasoner finds every suggested parent contradictory. |
| In only because of your axioms | 8 | Classes such as "Antibiotic" and "Hydrogel", which have a BFO type but no checked parent. |

**Why there are 45 relations, not 44.** One more had been dropped quietly before: "Negative Pressure Wound Therapy Dressing participates in Negative pressure wound therapy plan specification". It's flagged on the page as the one the reasoner finds contradictory. "Lower Extremity has part Lower Extremity" is flagged as pointing at itself.

**What the page can't show.** It only catches duplicates whose names match. "Burn Wound" next to WONT's "Thermal wound" isn't caught; you'll see it in the relations tab.

**A bug I found and fixed on the way.** The step that decides which new classes go into WONT compared parents by name only. When a parent came from another ontology under a slightly different name ("Bacterium" is "Bacteria" in NCBITaxon), 63 checked classes were left out. Bacterium's 42 children were the main case. They are in now. The new part of WONT has 3,016 new classes, and the ELK reasoner finds no contradictions.

**Other changes:**
- **Rule 72 in INSTRUCTIONS.md:** nothing goes into WONT that you haven't seen.
- **Figure and flow page:** both are updated: 3,016 new classes, and 45 relations waiting for you instead of 44 added.

**HermiT and JFact** are still running on the earlier version of these new classes. That version had more axioms than today's, so a clean result would also hold for the current one.

When you're done, tell me and I'll read your decisions back and apply them. The script for that doesn't exist yet; I'll write it from your actual decisions.

2026-10-06 15:29:10 · Claude
The duplicate-id bug is fixed. Every new class now gets the same WONT id however its name is capitalised.

**The cause.** For names with a capital after a hyphen, such as "Retro-Rectus" or "Complete-Thickness", the code that makes words singular mistook the second capital for an abbreviation. It then cut off the final "s" as if it were a plural ending: "Retro-Rectus" became "retrorectu", while "retro-rectus" stayed "retrorectus". The two spellings of one term didn't match.

**The fix.** These names are now made singular exactly like their lowercase spelling, and each part gets its capital back. "T-Cells" still becomes "T-Cell" and "Follow-Ups" becomes "Follow-Up". Abbreviations like "IL-6s" are handled as before.

**What it touched.** Of 143,659 names, 10 were affected:
- **Identifier list:** 5 entries had been filed under the cut-off form: Complete-Thickness, Extremity-Loss, Neo-Epidermis, Pro-Angiogenesis and Retro-Rectus. I added the correct form to each and kept the old one. All 7,212 new classes now get their own id back under both capitalisations, and no ids were merged or changed.
- **Terms:** no merging was needed. The capitalised spellings only ever existed as labels written into the ontology file; the evidence from the abstracts holds one spelling each. A dry run of the spelling clean-up confirms it finds nothing to rename.
- **Ontology:** the new part of WONT doesn't need rebuilding, because no id moved.

**Checks.**
- I added `tests/test_hyphen_title_case.py`; 8 of its 16 tests fail on the old code and all pass on the new.
- The full test suite passes: 1,885 tests. I left out the 5 integration tests that call the language model over the network. My first full run, which included them, stalled on one of them.
- The fix is noted under rule 71 in INSTRUCTIONS.md, with backups in `data/backup/normalise_hyphen_case_2026-10-06/`.

**Noticed, not changed.** "full thickness" is filed under "complete-thickness", not under WONT's own class "Full-thickness". It's already on your review page as a possible duplicate ("Complete-Thickness" vs "Full-thickness").

Your review page still has no decisions on it: https://claude.ai/artifact/V6Ejezr8tobgJeiwHgy8uM
